The deduplicator keeps\n33\t * the first arrival and silently drops duplicates, incrementing\n34\t * `droppedCount` for observability.\n35\t *\n36\t * Events with `sequence < 0` (legacy formats without sequence) are\n37\t * always passed through — they cannot be deduped.\n38\t */\n39\texport class EventDeduplicator {\n40\t  private readonly seen: Map<string, true> = new Map();\n41\t  private readonly maxSize: number;\n42\t  droppedCount = 0;\n43\t\n44\t  constructor(maxSize = 10_000) {\n45\t    this.maxSize = maxSize;\n46\t  }\n47\t\n48\t  /**\n49\t   * Returns `true` if this is the first occurrence (keep the event),\n50\t   * `false` if it is a duplicate (drop it).\n51\t   */\n52\t  check(event: NeedleEvent): boolean {\n53\t    if (event.sequence < 0) return true;\n54\t\n55\t    const key = `${event.session_id}\\0${event.worker_id}\\0${event.sequence}`;\n56\t    if (this.seen.has(key)) {\n57\t      this.droppedCount++;\n58\t      return false;\n59\t    }\n60\t\n61\t    this.seen.set(key, true);\n62\t\n63\t    // Evict oldest entries when over capacity\n64\t    if (this.seen.size > this.maxSize) {\n65\t      const excess = this.seen.size - this.maxSize;\n66\t      let count = 0;\n67\t      for (const k of this.seen.keys()) {\n68\t        if (count >= excess) break;\n69\t        this.seen.delete(k);\n70\t        count++;\n71\t      }\n72\t    }\n73\t\n74\t    return true;\n75\t  }\n76\t\n77\t  reset(): void {\n78\t    this.seen.clear();\n79\t    this.droppedCount = 0;\n80\t  }\n81\t\n82\t  get size(): number {\n83\t    return this.seen.size;\n84\t  }\n85\t}\n86\t\n87\t// ── Internal interfaces for legacy formats ────────────────────\n88\t\n89\tinterface NeedleWorkerObject {\n90\t  runner: string;\n91\t  provider: string;\n92\t  model: {\n155\t  // Accept pre-parsed objects directly\n156\t  let parsed: unknown;\n157\t  if (typeof raw === 'string') {\n158\t    if (!raw || !raw.trim()) return null;\n159\t    try {\n160\t      parsed = );\n227\t}\n228\t\n229\tfunction normalizeNeedleLogEntry(entry: NeedleLogEntry): NeedleEvent {\n230\t  const workerId = typeof entry.worker === 'string'\n231\t    ? entry.worker\n232\t    : * FABRIC Normalizer\n *\n * Normalizes raw events from multiple ingestion sources into the canonical\n * NeedleEvent shape. Each source (JSONL, OTLP) has its own parsing path,\n * but all produce the same output type so the downstream event bus sees\n * a uniform stream.\n */\n\nimport {\n  LogEvent,\n  LogLevel,\n  NeedleEvent,\n  NEEDLE_EVENT_SCHEMA_VERSION,\n} from './types.js';\n\n// ── Source types ──────────────────────────────────────────────\n\nexport type NormalizerSource =\n  | 'jsonl'\n  | 'otlp-log'\n  | 'otlp-span-start'\n  | 'otlp-span-end'\n  | 'otlp-metric';\n\n// ── Event deduplication ───────────────────────────────────────\n\n/**\n * LRU dedup set keyed on (session_id, worker_id, sequence).\n *\n * When FABRIC ingests events from both JSONL and OTLP sources,\n * the same logical event can arrive twice. The deduplicator keeps\n * the first arrival and silently drops duplicates, incrementing\n * `droppedCount` for observability.\n *\n * Events with `sequence < 0` (legacy formats without sequence) are\n * always passed through — they cannot be deduped.\n */\nexport class EventDeduplicator {\n  private readonly seen: Map<string, true> = new Map();\n  private readonly maxSize: number;\n  droppedCount = 0;\n\n  constructor(maxSize = 10_000) {\n    this.maxSize = maxSize;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Returns `true` if this is the first occurrence (keep the event),\n   * `false` if it is a duplicate (drop it).\n   */\n  check(event: NeedleEvent): boolean {\n    if (event.sequence < 0) return true;\n\n    const key = `${event.session_id}\\0${event.worker_id}\\0${event.sequence}`;\n    if (this.seen.has(key)) {\n      this.droppedCount++;\n      return false;\n    }\n\n    this.seen.set(key, true);\n\n    // Evict oldest entries when over capacity\n    if (this.seen.size > this.maxSize) {\n      const excess = this.seen.size - this.maxSize;\n      let count = 0;\n      for (const k of this.seen.keys()) {\n        if (count >= excess) break;\n        this.seen.delete(k);\n        count++;\n      }\n    }\n\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  reset(): void {\n    this.seen.clear();\n    this.droppedCount = 0;\n  }\n\n  get size(): number {\n    return this.seen.size;\n  }\n}\n\n// ── Internal interfaces for legacy formats ────────────────────\n\ninterface NeedleWorkerObject {\n  runner: string;\n  provider: string;\n  model: string;\n  identifier: string;\n}\n\ninterface NeedleLogEntry {\n  ts: string;\n  event: string;\n  level?: string;\n  session: string;\n  worker: string | NeedleWorkerObject;\n  data: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\n// ── Main entry point ──────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/**\n * Normalize a raw input into the canonical NeedleEvent shape.\n *\n * @param raw   Raw payload (string for JSONL, object for OTLP)\n * @param source Which ingestion source produced this input\n * @returns Normalized NeedleEvent, or null if the input is invalid/unrecognized\n */\nexport function normalize(\n  raw: string | unknown,\n  source: NormalizerSource,\n): NeedleEvent | null {\n  switch (source) {\n    case 'jsonl':\n      return normalizeJsonl(raw as string);\n    case 'otlp-log':\n      return normalizeOtlpLog(raw);\n    case 'otlp-span-start':\n      return normalizeOtlpSpanStart(raw);\n    case 'otlp-span-end':\n      return normalizeOtlpSpanEnd(raw);\n    case 'otlp-metric':\n      return normalizeOtlpMetric(raw);\n    default:\n      return null;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Convenience: normalize and then convert to the legacy LogEvent shape\n * used by the existing store / TUI / web consumers.\n *\n * When a `deduplicator` is provided, the NeedleEvent is checked against it\n * before conversion. Duplicates return null (caller should skip).\n */\nexport function normalizeToLogEvent(\n  raw: string | unknown,\n  source: NormalizerSource,\n  deduplicator?: EventDeduplicator,\n): LogEvent | null {\n  const ne = normalize(raw, source);\n  if (!ne) return null;\n  if (deduplicator && !deduplicator.check(ne)) return null;\n  return needleEventToLogEvent(ne);\n}\n\n// ── JSONL source Namespaced keys\n * take priority when present.\n *\n *   Namespaced:  needle.worker.id, needle.session.id,\n *                needle.sequence,   needle.bead.id\n *   Fallback:    worker_id, session_id, sequence, bead_id\n *   Shared:      event_type  (never namespaced)\n *\n * The OTLP `body` field (AnyValue) is lifted to `data`. value: Record<string, unknown> }> }).values) {\n      out[kv.key] = unwrapAnyValue(kv.value);\n    }\n    return out;\n  }\n\n  // Protobuf JSON scalar wrappers: { stringValue: \"…\" }, { intValue: 5 }, etc.\n  if ('stringValue' in b || 'intValue' in b || 'doubleValue' in b || 'boolValue' in b) {\n    return { value: unwrapAnyValue(b) };\n  }\n\n  // Plain JSON object (OTLP/HTTP JSON mode)\n  return { ...(b as Record<string, unknown>) };\n}\n\n/** Unwrap an OTLP AnyValue to a JS primitive / object. */\nfunction unwrapAnyValue(v: Record<string, unknown>): unknown {\n  if ('stringValue' in v) return v.stringValue;\n  if ('intValue'    in v) return Number(v.intValue);\n  if ('doubleValue' in v) return v.doubleValue;\n  if ('boolValue'   in v) return v.boolValue;\n  if ('arrayValue'  in v && Array.isArray((v.arrayValue as Record<string, unknown>)?.values)) {\n    return ((v.arrayValue as { values: Array<Record<string, unknown>> }).values)\n      .map(item => unwrapAnyValue(item));\n  }\n  if ('kvlistValue' in v && Array.isArray((v.kvlistValue as Record<string, unknown>)?.values)) {\n    const out: Record<string, unknown> = {};\n    for (const kv of ((v.kvlistValue as { values: Array<{ key: string; attrs = record.attributes;\n\n  if (Array.isArray(attrs)) {\n    for (const attr of attrs) {\n      if (typeof attr !== 'object' || attr === null) continue;\n      const a = attr as Record<string, unknown>;\n      const key = a.key as string | undefined;\n      if (!key) continue;\n      const val = a.value as Record<string, unknown> | undefined;\n      if (!val) continue;\n      if ('stringValue' in val) map.set(key, val.stringValue);\n      else if ('intValue' in val) map.set(key, Number(val.intValue));\n      else if ('doubleValue' in val) map.set(key, val.doubleValue);\n      else if ('boolValue' in val) map.set(key, val.boolValue);\n    }\n  } else if (typeof attrs === 'object' && attrs !== null) {\n    for (const [key, val] of Object.entries(attrs as Record<string, unknown>)) {\n      map.set(key, val);\n    }\n  }\n\n  return map;\n}\n\nfunction otlpNanoToISO(nano: string | number): string {\n  const ms = Math.round(Number(nano) / 1_000_000);\n  return new 'bead.mitosis.check'\n86\t  | 'bead.mitosis.started'\n87\t  | 'bead.mitosis.child_created'\n88\t  | 'bead.mitosis.complete'\n89\t  | 'bead.mitosis.failed'\n90\t  | 'bead.mitosis.skipped'\n91\t  // Strand lifecycle\n92\t  | 'strand.started'\n93\t  | 'strand.completed'\n94\t  | 'strand.fallthrough'\n95\t  | 'strand.skipped'\n96\t  // Hook lifecycle\n97\t  | 'hook.started'\n98\t  | 'hook.completed'\n99\t  | 'hook.failed'\n100\t  // Heartbeat\n101\t  | 'heartbeat.emitted'\n102\t  | 'heartbeat.stuck_detected'\n103\t  | 'heartbeat.recovery'\n104\t  // Mend (maintenance)\n105\t  | 'mend.orphan_released'\n106\t  | 'mend.heartbeat_cleaned'\n107\t  | 'mend.logs_pruned'\n108\t  | 'mend.completed'\n109\t  // Unravel (alternatives)\n110\t  | 'unravel.alternatives_created'\n111\t  | 'unravel.alternative_created'\n112\t  | 'unravel.analysis_started'\n113\t  | 'unravel.analysis_completed'\n114\t  // Weave (documentation gaps)\n115\t  | 'weave.bead_created'\n116\t  | 'weave.analysis_started'\n117\t  | 'weave.analysis_completed'\n118\t  // Pulse (health monitoring)\n119\t  | 'pulse.bead_created'\n120\t  | 'pulse.scan_started'\n121\t  | 'pulse.scan_completed'\n122\t  | 'pulse.issue_detected'\n123\t  | 'pulse.detector_started'\n124\t  | 'pulse.detector_completed'\n125\t  // Error events\n126\t  | 'error.claim_failed'\n127\t  | 'error.agent_crash'\n128\t  | 'error.timeout'\n129\t  | 'error.release_failed'\n130\t  // Effort & budget\n131\t  | 'effort.recorded'\n132\t  | 'budget.warning'\n133\t  | 'budget.exceeded'\n134\t  | 'budget.per_bead_exceeded'\n135\t  // File locks\n136\t  | 'file.checkout'\n137\t  | 'file.conflict'\n138\t  | 'file.release'\n139\t  | 'file.stale'\n140\t  | 'lock.priority_bump'\n141\t  | 'lock.priority_bump_received'\n142\t  | 'lock.expired';\n143\t\n144\t// ============================================\n145\t// Canonical NeedleEvent (wire schema)\n146\t// ============================================\n147\t\n148\t/**\n149\t * Canonical event shape emitted by NEEDLE over JSONL and OTLP.\n150\t * This is the contract between NEEDLE and FABRIC — versioned via NEEDLE_EVENT_SCHEMA_VERSION.\n151\t *\n152\t * Ordering: sort by (worker_id, sequence), NOT by timestamp.\n153\t * Wall clocks skew across hosts; sequence is the worker's monotonic counter.\n154\t */\n155\texport interface NeedleEvent {\n156\t  /** RFC3339 timestamp — display only, NOT authoritative for ordering */\n157\t  timestamp: string;\n158\t\n159\t  /** Event taxonomy string, e.g. \"worker.started\", \"bead.claimed\" */\n160\t  event_type: NeedleEventType | string;\n161\t\n162\t  /** Worker identifier (e.g. \"tcb-alpha\") */\n163\t  worker_id: string;\n164\t\n165\t  /** Session identifier grouping a worker's lifetime events */\n166\t  session_id: string;\n167\t\n168\t  /** Per-worker monotonic counter — authoritative for ordering */\n169\t  sequence: number;\n170\t\n171\t  /** Present when event pertains to a specific bead */\n172\t  bead_id?: string;\n173\t\n174\t  /** Event-specific payload */\n175\t  data: Record<string, unknown>;\n176\t}\n177\t\n178\t// ============================================\n179\t// Conversation Event Types\n180\t// ============================================\n181\t\n182\t/**\n183\t * Role in a conversation\n184\t */\n185\texport type ConversationRole = 'system' | 'user' | 'assistant' | 'tool';\n186\t\n187\t/**\n188\t * Type of conversation event\n189\t */\n190\texport type ConversationEventType =\n191\t  | 'prompt'        // User input/prompt\n192\t  | 'response'      // Assistant response text\n193\t  | 'thinking'      // Internal reasoning/thinking block\n194\t  | 'tool_call'     // Tool being called with arguments\n195\t  | 'tool_result';  // Result from a tool call\n196\t\n197\t/**\n198\t * Base interface for all conversation events\n199\t */\n200\texport interface ConversationEventBase {\n201\t  /** Unique event identifier */\n202\t  id: string;\n203\t\n204\t  /** Type of conversation event */\n205\t  type: ConversationEventType;\n206\t\n207\t  /** Role in conversation */\n208\t  role: ConversationRole;\n209\t\n210\t  /** Unix timestamp in milliseconds */\n211\t  ts: number;\n212\t\n213\t  /** Worker identifier */\n214\t  worker: string;\n215\t\n216\t  /** Associated bead/task ID (if any) */\n217\t  bead?: string;\n218\t\n219\t  /** Sequence number within the conversation */\n220\t  sequence: number;\n221\t\n222\t  /** Token count for this event (if available) */\n223\t  tokens?: number;\n224\t}\n225\t\n226\t/**\n227\t * User prompt event\n228\t */\n229\texport interface PromptEvent extends ConversationEventBase {\n230\t  type: 'prompt';\n231\t  role: 'user';\n232\t\n233\t  /** The user's prompt text */\n234\t  content: string;\n235\t\n236\t  /** Whether this is a continuation of a previous prompt */\n237\t  isContinuation?: boolean;\n238\t}\n239\t\n240\t/**\n241\t * Assistant response event\n242\t */\n243\texport interface ResponseEvent extends ConversationEventBase {\n244\t  type: 'response';\n245\t  role: 'assistant';\n246\t\n247\t  /** The response text */\n248\t  content: string;\n249\t\n250\t  /** Whether the response is truncated */\n251\t  isTruncated?: boolean;\n252\t\n253\t  /** Model used for this response */\n254\t  model?: string;\n255\t\n256\t  /** Stop reason (if available) */\n257\t  stopReason?: 'end_turn' | 'max_tokens' | 'stop_sequence' | 'tool_use';\n258\t}\n259\t\n260\t/**\n261\t * Thinking/reasoning block event\n262\t */\n263\texport interface ThinkingEvent extends ConversationEventBase {\n264\t  type: 'thinking';\n265\t  role: 'assistant';\n266\t\n267\t  /** The thinking content */\n268\t  content: string;\n269\t\n270\t  /** Whether thinking is truncated */\n271\t  isTruncated?: boolean;\n272\t\n273\t  /** Duration of thinking in ms (if available) */\n274\t  durationMs?: number;\n275\t}\n276\t\n277\t/**\n278\t * Tool argument types\n279\t */\n280\texport type ToolArgValue = string | number | boolean | null | ToolArgValue[] | { [key: string]: ToolArgValue };\n281\t\n282\t/**\n283\t * Tool call event\n284\t */\n285\texport interface ToolCallEvent extends ConversationEventBase {\n286\t  type: 'tool_call';\n287\t  role: 'assistant';\n288\t\n289\t  /** Tool name */\n290\t  tool: string;\n291\t\n292\t  /** Tool arguments */\n293\t  args: Record<string, ToolArgValue>;\n294\t\n295\t  /** Tool call ID (for correlating with results) */\n296\t  toolCallId?: string;\n297\t\n298\t  /** Human-readable summary of the call */\n299\t  summary?: string;\n300\t}\n301\t\n302\t/**\n303\t * Tool result event\n304\t */\n305\texport interface ToolResultEvent extends ConversationEventBase {\n306\t  type: 'tool_result';\n307\t  role: 'tool';\n308\t\n309\t  /** Tool name */\n310\t  tool: string;\n311\t\n312\t  /** Tool call ID this is a response to */\n313\t  toolCallId?: string;\n314\t\n315\t  /** Result content (may be truncated) */\n316\t  content: string;\n317\t\n318\t  /** Whether the tool call succeeded */\n319\t  success: boolean;\n320\t\n321\t  /** Error message if failed */\n322\t  error?: string;\n323\t\n324\t  /** Duration of tool call in ms */\n325\t  durationMs?: number;\n326\t\n327\t  /** Whether the result is truncated */\n328\t  isTruncated?: boolean;\n329\t\n330\t  /** Size of full result in bytes (for context) */\n331\t  resultSize?: number;\n332\t}\n333\t\n334\t/**\n335\t * Union type for all conversation events\n336\t */\n337\texport type ConversationEvent =\n338\t  | PromptEvent\n339\t  | ResponseEvent\n340\t  | ThinkingEvent\n341\t  | ToolCallEvent\n342\t  | ToolResultEvent;\n343\t\n344\t/**\n345\t * A complete conversation session\n346\t */\n347\texport interface ConversationSession {\n348\t  /** Session identifier */\n349\t  id: string;\n350\t\n351\t  /** Worker ID */\n352\t  workerId: string;\n353\t\n354\t  /** Associated bead ID (if any) */\n355\t  beadId?: string;\n356\t\n357\t  /** Start timestamp */\n358\t  startTime: number;\n359\t\n360\t  /** End timestamp (if complete) */\n361\t  endTime?: number;\n362\t\n363\t  /** All events in chronological order */\n364\t  events: ConversationEvent[];\n365\t\n366\t  /** Total token count */\n367\t  totalTokens: number;\n368\t\n369\t  /** Number of turns */\n370\t  turnCount: number;\n371\t\n372\t  /** Tools used in this session */\n373\t  toolsUsed: string[];\n374\t\n375\t  /** Whether the session is still active */\n376\t  isActive: boolean;\n377\t}\n378\t\n379\t/**\n380\t * Options for parsing conversation events\n381\t */\n382\texport interface ConversationParseOptions {\n383\t  /** Maximum content length before truncation */\n384\t  maxContentLength?: number;\n385\t\n386\t  /** Include thinking blocks */\n387\t  includeThinking?: boolean;\n388\t\n389\t  /** Include tool results */\n390\t  includeToolResults?: boolean;\n391\t\n392\t  /** Truncate tool results longer than this */\n393\t  maxToolResultLength?: number;\n394\t}\n395\t\n396\t// ============================================\n397\t// Core Log Event Types\n398\t// ============================================\n399\t\n400\texport interface LogEvent {\n401\t  /** Unix timestamp in milliseconds — display only, NOT authoritative for ordering */\n402\t  ts: number;\n403\t\n404\t  /** Worker identifier (e.g., 'w-abc123') */\n405\t  worker: string;\n406\t\n407\t  /** Per-worker monotonic counter — authoritative for ordering (from NeedleEvent.sequence) */\n408\t  sequence?: number;\n409\t\n410\t  /** Log level */\n411\t  level: LogLevel;\n412\t\n413\t  /** Log message */\n414\t  msg: string;\n415\t\n416\t  /** Optional: Tool that was called */\n417\t  tool?: string;\n418\t\n419\t  /** Optional: File path being operated on */\n420\t  path?: string;\n421\t\n422\t  /** Optional: Bead/task identifier */\n423\t  bead?: string;\n424\t\n425\t  /** Optional: Duration in milliseconds */\n426\t  duration_ms?: number;\n427\t\n428\t  /** Optional: Error details */\n429\t  error?: string;\n430\t\n431\t  /** NEEDLE session identifier (e.g. 'needle-claude-anthropic-sonnet-alpha') */\n432\t  session?: string;\n433\t\n434\t  /** AI provider extracted from NEEDLE worker string (e.g. 'anthropic', 'openai') */\n435\t  provider?: string;\n436\t\n437\t  /** AI model identifier extracted from NEEDLE worker string (e.g. 'sonnet', 'gpt-4o') */\n438\t  model?: string;\n439\t\n440\t  /** Any additional fields */\n441\t  [key: string]: unknown;\n442\t}\n443\t\n444\t/**\n445\t * Compare two LogEvents by (worker, sequence), falling back to ts.\n446\t *\n447\t * Sequence is the authoritative ordering key within a worker (monotonic counter\n448\t * from NEEDLE). Timestamp is used only as a display fallback for legacy events\n449\t * that lack a sequence number.\n450\t */\n451\texport function compareEventsBySequence(a: LogEvent, b: LogEvent): number {\n452\t  const seqA = a.sequence != null && a.sequence >= 0 ? a.sequence : null;\n453\t  const seqB = b.sequence != null && b.sequence >= 0 ? b.sequence : null;\n454\t\n455\t  if (seqA !== null && seqB !== null) {\n456\t    if (a.worker !== b.worker) return a.worker.localeCompare(b.worker);\n457\t    return seqA - seqB;\n458\t  }\n459\t\n460\t  return a.ts - b.ts;\n461\t}\n462\t\n463\texport interface WorkerInfo {\n464\t  /** Worker identifier */\n465\t  id: string;\n466\t\n467\t  /** Current status (coarse — derived from needleState) */\n468\t  status: WorkerStatus;\n469\t\n470\t  /** Current NEEDLE state machine state (fine-grained) */\n471\t  needleState?: NeedleState;\n472\t\n473\t  /** Timestamp of the last state transition (ms since epoch) */\n474\t  lastStateTransition?: number;\n475\t\n476\t  /** Last event received */\n477\t  lastEvent?: LogEvent;\n478\t\n479\t  /** Total beads completed */\n480\t  beadsCompleted: number;\n481\t\n482\t  /** First seen timestamp */\n483\t  firstSeen: number;\n484\t\n485\t  /** Last activity timestamp */\n486\t  lastActivity: number;\n487\t\n488\t  /** Files currently being modified by this worker */\n489\t  activeFiles: string[];\n490\t\n491\t  /** Whether this worker is involved in any collisions */\n492\t  hasCollision: boolean;\n493\t\n494\t  /** Current bead/task being worked on */\n495\t  activeBead?: string;\n496\t\n497\t  /** Directories this worker is active in */\n498\t  activeDirectories: string[];\n499\t\n500\t  /** All collision types this worker is involved in */\n501\t  collisionTypes: ('file' | 'bead' | 'task')[];\n502\t\n503\t  /** Total number of events received for this worker */\n504\t  eventCount: number;\n505\t\n506\t  /** Whether the worker appears stuck (gap-based detection) */\n507\t  stuck?: boolean;\n508\t\n509\t  /** Human-readable reason the worker is stuck */\n510\t  stuckReason?: string;\n511\t}\n512\t\n513\texport interface EventFilter {\n514\t  /** Filter by worker ID */\n515\t  worker?: string;\n516\t\n517\t  /** Filter by log level */\n518\t  level?: LogLevel;\n519\t\n520\t  /** Filter by bead ID */\n521\t  bead?: string;\n522\t\n523\t  /** Filter by event type (glob pattern, e.g. \"bead.*\") */\n524\t  eventType?: string;\n525\t\n526\t  /** Filter by file path */\n527\t  path?: string;\n528\t\n529\t  /** Time range start (Unix timestamp) */\n530\t  since?: number;\n531\t\n532\t  /** Time range end (Unix timestamp) */\n533\t  until?: number;\n534\t}\n535\t\n536\t/**\n537\t * File collision event - when multiple workers modify the same file concurrently\n538\t */\n539\texport interface FileCollision {\n540\t  /** File path being contested */\n541\t  path: string;\n542\t\n543\t  /** Workers involved in the collision */\n544\t  workers: string[];\n545\t\n546\t  /** Timestamp when collision was detected */\n547\t  detectedAt: number;\n548\t\n549\t  /** Events that triggered the collision */\n550\t  events: LogEvent[];\n551\t\n552\t  /** Whether the collision is still active */\n553\t  isActive: boolean;\n554\t}\n555\t\n556\t/**\n557\t * Bead collision - when multiple workers work on the same bead/task\n558\t */\n559\texport interface BeadCollision {\n560\t  /** Bead ID being contested */\n561\t  beadId: string;\n562\t\n563\t  /** Workers working on this bead */\n564\t  workers: string[];\n565\t\n566\t  /** Timestamp when collision was detected */\n567\t  detectedAt: number;\n568\t\n569\t  /** Events that triggered the collision */\n570\t  events: LogEvent[];\n571\t\n572\t  /** Whether the collision is still active */\n573\t  isActive: boolean;\n574\t\n575\t  /** Collision severity based on operation types */\n576\t  severity: 'warning' | 'critical';\n577\t}\n578\t\n579\t/**\n580\t * Task collision - when workers work on tasks that may conflict\n581\t */\n582\texport interface TaskCollision {\n583\t  /** Type of collision */\n584\t  type: 'directory' | 'related_files' | 'dependency';\n585\t\n586\t  /** Human-readable description */\n587\t  description: string;\n588\t\n589\t  /** Workers involved */\n590\t  workers: string[];\n591\t\n592\t  /** Affected paths/beads */\n593\t  affectedResources: string[];\n594\t\n595\t  /** Timestamp when collision was detected */\n596\t  detectedAt: number;\n597\t\n598\t  /** Whether the collision is still active */\n599\t  isActive: boolean;\n600\t\n601\t  /** Risk level */\n602\t  riskLevel: 'low' | 'medium' | 'high';\n603\t}\n604\t\n605\t/**\n606\t * Collision alert for user notification\n607\t */\n608\texport interface CollisionAlert {\n609\t  /** Unique alert ID */\n610\t  id: string;\n611\t\n612\t  /** Alert type */\n613\t  type: 'file' | 'bead' | 'task';\n614\t\n615\t  /** Severity level */\n616\t  severity: 'info' | 'warning' | 'error' | 'critical';\n617\t\n618\t  /** Human-readable title */\n619\t  title: string;\n620\t\n621\t  /** Detailed description */\n622\t  description: string;\n623\t\n624\t  /** Workers involved */\n625\t  workers: string[];\n626\t\n627\t  /** Timestamp when alert was generated */\n628\t  timestamp: number;\n629\t\n630\t  /** Whether the alert has been acknowledged */\n631\t  acknowledged: boolean;\n632\t\n633\t  /** Related collision data */\n634\t  collision: FileCollision | BeadCollision | TaskCollision;\n635\t\n636\t  /** Suggested resolution */\n637\t  suggestion?: string;\n638\t}\n639\t\n640\texport interface EventStore {\n641\t  /** Add an event to the store */\n642\t  add(event: LogEvent): void;\n643\t\n644\t  /** Query events with optional filter */\n645\t  query(filter?: EventFilter): LogEvent[];\n646\t\n647\t  /** Query events sorted by (worker, sequence), falling back to */\n654\t  getWorkers(): WorkerInfo[];\n655\t\n656\t  /** Clear all events */\n657\t  clear(): void;\n658\t\n659\t  /** Get all active collisions */\n660\t  getCollisions(): ============================================\n667\t// Error Grouping Types\n668\t// ============================================\n669\t\n670\t/**\n671\t * Error fingerprint - used to identify similar errors\n672\t */\n673\texport interface ErrorFingerprint {\n674\t  /** Normalized pattern signature (e.g., \"ECONNREFUSED_*:*\" for connection errors) */\n675\t  signature: string;\n676\t\n677\t  /** Category of error (network, permission, validation, etc.) */\n678\t  category: ErrorCategory;\n679\t\n680\t  /** Original error message (first occurrence) */\n681\t  sampleMessage: string;\n682\t\n683\t  /** Hash of the signature for quick comparison */\n684\t  hash: string;\n685\t}\n686\t\n687\t/**\n688\t * Error categories for grouping\n689\t */\n690\texport type ErrorCategory =\n691\t  | 'network'      // Connection errors, timeouts, DNS issues\n692\t  | 'permission'   // Auth failures, access denied\n693\t  | 'validation'   // Invalid input, schema errors\n694\t  | 'resource'     // Out of memory, disk full, quota exceeded\n695\t  | 'not_found'    // File not found, 404 errors\n696\t  | 'timeout'      // Operation timed out\n697\t  | 'syntax'       // Parse errors, malformed data\n698\t  | 'tool'         // Tool-specific failures\n699\t  | 'unknown';     // Uncategorized errors\n700\t\n701\t/**\n702\t * Grouped error - clusters similar errors together\n703\t */\n704\texport interface ErrorGroup {\n705\t  /** Unique group ID */\n706\t  id: string;\n707\t\n708\t  /** Fingerprint identifying this group */\n709\t  fingerprint: ErrorFingerprint;\n710\t\n711\t  /** All error events in this group */\n712\t  events: LogEvent[];\n713\t\n714\t  /** First occurrence timestamp */\n715\t  firstSeen: number;\n716\t\n717\t  /** Most recent occurrence timestamp */\n718\t  lastSeen: number;\n719\t\n720\t  /** Number of occurrences */\n721\t  count: number;\n722\t\n723\t  /** Workers that have encountered this error */\n724\t  affectedWorkers: string[];\n725\t\n726\t  /** Whether this error group is currently active (seen recently) */\n727\t  isActive: boolean;\n728\t\n729\t  /** Severity based on frequency and recency */\n730\t  severity: 'low' | 'medium' | 'high' | 'critical';\n731\t}\n732\t\n733\t/**\n734\t * Options for error grouping\n735\t */\n736\texport interface ErrorGroupingOptions {\n737\t  /** Time window to consider errors as active (ms), default 5 minutes */\n738\t  activeWindowMs?: number;\n739\t\n740\t  /** Minimum occurrences for high severity */\n741\t  highSeverityThreshold?: number;\n742\t\n743\t  /** Minimum occurrences for critical severity */\n744\t  criticalSeverityThreshold?: number;\n745\t\n746\t  /** Maximum groups to track */\n747\t  maxGroups?: number;\n748\t}\n749\t\n750\t// ============================================\n751\t// Session Replay Types\n752\t// ============================================\n753\t\n754\texport type ReplaySpeed = 0.5 | 1 | 2 | 5 | 10;\n755\t\n756\texport type ReplayState = 'idle' | 'playing' | 'paused' | 'ended';\n757\t\n758\texport interface ReplaySession {\n759\t  /** Unique session identifier */\n760\t  id: string;\n761\t\n762\t  /** Source log file path */\n763\t  sourcePath: string;\n764\t\n765\t  /** Total events in the session */\n766\t  totalEvents: number;\n767\t\n768\t  /** Current playback position (event index) */\n769\t  currentIndex: number;\n770\t\n771\t  /** Playback state */\n772\t  state: ReplayState;\n773\t\n774\t  /** Playback speed multiplier */\n775\t  speed: number;\n782\t\n783\t  /** Filter applied during replay */\n784\t  filter?: EventFilter;\n785\t}\n786\t\n787\texport interface ReplayControls {\n788\t  /** Start or resume playback */\n789\t  play(): void;\n790\t\n791\t  /** Pause playback */\n792\t  pause(): void;\n793\t\n794\t  /** Toggle play/pause */\n795\t  toggle(): void;\n796\t\n797\t  /** Step to next event */\n798\t  stepForward(): void;\n799\t\n800\t  /** Step to previous event */\n801\t  stepBackward(): void;\n802\t\n803\t  /** Jump to specific event index */\n804\t  seekTo(index: number): void;\n805\t\n806\t  /** Set playback speed */\n807\t  setSpeed(speed: ReplaySpeed): void;\n808\t\n809\t  /** Stop and reset replay */\n810\t  reset(): void;\n811\t}\n812\t\n813\t// ============================================\n814\t// File Heatmap Types\n815\t// ============================================\n816\t\n817\t/**\n818\t * Heat level for a file based on modification frequency\n819\t */\n820\texport type HeatLevel = 'cold' | 'warm' | 'hot' | 'critical';\n821\t\n822\t/**\n823\t * Worker contribution to a file's modification history\n824\t */\n825\texport interface WorkerFileContribution {\n826\t  /** Worker ID */\n827\t */\n868\t  avgModificationInterval: number;\n869\t}\n870\t\n871\t/**\n872\t * Options for heatmap generation\n873\t */\n874\texport interface HeatmapOptions {\n875\t  /** Minimum modifications to be /** Only show files with collisions */\n888\t  collisionsOnly?: boolean;\n889\t}\n890\t\n891\t/**\n892\t * Statistics for the entire file heatmap\n893\t */\n894\texport interface FileHeatmapStats number;\n915\t}\n916\t\n917\t// ============================================\n918\t// File Anomaly Detection Types\n919\t// ============================================\n920\t\n921\t/**\n922\t * Types * FABRIC Type Definitions\n *\n * Core types for NEEDLE log parsing and worker state management.\n */\n\n/**\n * Schema version for the NEEDLE event wire format.\n * Bumped when the canonical NeedleEvent shape changes.\n * Both NEEDLE and FABRIC must agree on this version.\n */\nexport const NEEDLE_EVENT_SCHEMA_VERSION = 1;\n\nexport type LogLevel = 'debug' | 'info' | 'warn' | 'error';\n\nexport type WorkerStatus = 'active' | 'idle' | 'error';\n\n/**\n * NEEDLE worker state machine — first-class states emitted by\n * worker.state_transition events. These are the canonical states\n * that replace the coarse WorkerStatus in the UI.\n *\n * BOOTING → SELECTING → CLAIMING → WORKING → CLOSING → STOPPED\n */\nexport type NeedleState =\n  | 'BOOTING'\n  | 'SELECTING'\n  | 'CLAIMING'\n  | 'WORKING'\n  | 'CLOSING'\n  | 'STOPPED';\n\n/**\n * All valid state transitions in the NEEDLE worker state machine.\n * A worker in BOOTING can only go to SELECTING, etc.\n */\nexport const VALID_TRANSITIONS: Record<NeedleState, NeedleState[]> = {\n  BOOTING:   ['SELECTING'],\n  SELECTING: ['CLAIMING', 'STOPPED'],\n  CLAIMING:  ['WORKING', 'SELECTING'],\n  WORKING:   ['CLOSING', 'SELECTING'],\n  CLOSING:   ['SELECTING', 'STOPPED'],\n  STOPPED:   ['BOOTING'],\n};\n\n/**\n * Map a NeedleState to the coarser WorkerStatus for backward compatibility.\n */\nexport function needleStateToStatus(state: NeedleState): WorkerStatus {\n  switch (state) {\n    case 'BOOTING':\n    case 'SELECTING':\n    case 'CLAIMING':\n    case 'WORKING':\n      return 'active';\n    case 'CLOSING':\n      return 'active';\n    case 'STOPPED':\n      return 'idle';\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * All event types emitted by NEEDLE's telemetry pipeline.\n * Format: category.action — matches NEEDLE's _needle_telemetry_emit event_type argument.\n */\nexport type NeedleEventType =\n  // Worker lifecycle\n  | 'worker.started'\n  | 'worker.idle'\n  | Event-specific payload */\n  data: Record<string, unknown>;\n}\n\n// ============================================\n// Conversation Event Types\n// ============================================\n\n/**\n
